Use when modeling currency exchange networks, detecting arbitrage opportunities via negative cycles, finding optimal trading routes, calculating path lengths, analyzing network topology, or working with any connected data structures. Supports Bellman-Ford, Dijkstra, and other graph algorithms. ([honeyvig/skillsbench](https://github.com/honeyvig/skillsbench/tree/HEAD/shared_workspace/forex-arbitrage-detector/environment/skills/networkx))
- **networkx**: NetworkX library for graph and network analysis. Use for creating graphs, calculating centrality metrics, detecting communities, and analyzing drug-drug interaction networks. ([honeyvig/skillsbench](https://github.com/honeyvig/skillsbench/tree/HEAD/shared_workspace/drug-interaction-network/environment/skills/networkx))
